This article describes the system architecture, core workflows, and benchmarks that reach literature performance for quantum mechanical, physicochemical and bioactivity property prediction, and use cases involving time series datasets demonstrating applications beyond molecular chemistry datasets.

</details>


### [153] [SpecRoll: Fast-Slow Verifier-Feedback Adaptation for Speculative Reinforcement Learning Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04962)
*Nhat Minh Pham,Duy Tung Doan,Thi Duyen Ngo,Vinh Van Nguyen,Khac-Hoai Nam Bui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpecRoll 是一种推测性推出生成引擎，通过双时尺度自适应机制在强化学习后训练中加速大语言模型的自回归生成，同时保持目标策略的采样分布和 GRPO 目标不变。它结合了轻量级未来 token 头的快速并行提议、基于延迟验证器反馈的 Reflex 模块进行无反向传播的隐状态局部修正、以及仅在检测到持续性能下降时更新头参数的慢速路径，配合并发感知稀疏树验证和精确目标验证。实验显示在 1.5B-14B 五个模型和三个数学推理数据集上，相比原版 GRPO 实现 1.26-2.15 倍生成加速和 1.21-2.04 倍端到端加速，且在所有 15 个匹配设置中均优于 FastGRPO，平均端到端增益 1.18 倍。消融实验证实快慢双路径互补有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习后训练虽能提升大语言模型推理能力，但自回归推出生成是主要效率瓶颈。推测性解码可加速生成，但在 RL 中难以直接应用：目标策略持续演化导致静态提议器失效，而频繁更新草稿模型会带来巨大开销。需要一种能适应策略演化、保持目标采样分布、且开销可控的推测性推出生成方案。

Method: 提出 SpecRoll 推测性推出生成引擎，核心机制包括：1) 双时尺度自适应：轻量级未来 token 头并行生成提议（快速路径），Reflex 模块利用延迟验证器反馈进行有界、轨迹局部的隐状态修正且无需反向传播；2) 慢速路径仅在检测到持续性能下降时更新头参数；3) 并发感知稀疏树验证与精确目标验证相结合，保证目标推出生成分布和 GRPO 目标函数不变。

Result: 在 5 个模型（1.5B-14B）和 3 个数学推理数据集上，SpecRoll 相比原版 GRPO 实现 1.26-2.15 倍生成速度提升和 1.21-2.04 倍端到端速度提升；在所有 15 个匹配设置中均优于 FastGRPO，平均端到端成对增益 1.18 倍。消融实验表明快速路径（Reflex 修正）和慢速路径（头参数更新）提供互补收益。

Conclusion: SpecRoll 通过双时尺度自适应推测性推出生成，有效解决了 RL 后训练中自回归生成的效率瓶颈，在保持目标策略分布和训练目标不变的前提下显著加速生成，且优于现有基线方法，为大规模 RL 后训练提供了实用的加速方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models, but autoregressive rollout generation remains a major efficiency bottleneck. Speculative decoding can accelerate generation, yet applying it during RL is difficult because the target policy continually evolves: static proposers become stale, while frequent drafter updates add substantial overhead. We introduce SpecRoll, a speculative rollout engine that preserves the target model's sampling distribution while adapting at two timescales. Lightweight future-token heads generate parallel proposals, while our proposed Reflex module uses delayed verifier feedback to perform bounded, trajectory-local hidden-state corrections without backpropagation. A complementary slow path updates the head parameters only when sustained degradation is detected. SpecRoll combines these mechanisms with concurrency-aware sparse-tree verification and exact target verification, leaving the target rollout distribution and GRPO objective unchanged. Across five models ranging from 1.5B to 14B and three mathematical reasoning datasets, SpecRoll achieves 1.26-2.15x generation speedup and 1.21-2.04x end-to-end speedup over vanilla GRPO. It also outperforms FastGRPO in both generation and end-to-end time across all 15 matched settings, with an average pairwise end-to-end gain of 1.18x. Controlled ablations show that the fast and slow adaptation paths provide complementary benefits. Our source code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SpecRoll-26062006.

</details>


### [154] [EvolveNet: Collaborative Harness Evolution for Agent Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04968)
*Jun Nie,Yonggang Zhang,Qianshu Cai,Yiu-ming Cheung,Xinmei Tian,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EvolveNet是一种协同进化框架，通过将共享工具链广播到本地部署，各自在私有工作负载上进化，仅聚合程序适配而非原始数据，解决了LLM智能体生态中经验无法集中化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法假设所有执行经验可路由到单一优化器，顺序进化单一工具链；但现实中用户、组织、环境生成隔离的经验流无法汇聚，最有价值的经验恰恰无法直接中心化。

Method: EvolveNet将经验提取移至数据端：广播共享工具链到本地智能体部署，各自在私有工作负载上进化；仅将程序适配组合成更新的共享工具链并重新分发。引入作用域类型的、证据引导的程序聚合机制，解决独立修改的程序无法像模型参数求平均且组合时可能冲突的问题。

Result: 在文本转SQL、数据科学编程、竞赛编程、软件工程、智能体工作流五个设置中，EvolveNet均提升了共享工具链，异构工作负载下增益最大；消融实验归因于不同智能体适配的组合而非选择。

Conclusion: EvolveNet通过将聚合边界从原始工作负载转移到已学习的适配，保持工作负载本地化，支持并发进化搜索并降低串行深度，实现了协同工具链进化范式。

Abstract: The capabilities of an LLM agent depend not only on its model but on the harness: the executable program that constructs context, invokes tools, verifies results, and recovers from failure. Recent work shows that evolving the harness yields persistent improvements without updating model weights. Existing approaches, however, assume that all execution experience can be routed to a single optimizer, which evolves one harness along a sequential trajectory. Real agent ecosystems violate that assumption: users, organizations, and environments generate isolated streams of experience that cannot be pooled, so the experience most worth learning from is exactly the experience that cannot be directly centralized. We introduce EvolveNet, a paradigm of collaborative harness evolution that moves experience extraction to the data. A shared harness is broadcast to data-local agent deployments, each of which evolves it on its own workload. Only the resulting program adaptations are composed into an updated shared harness and redistributed, so that every participating agent inherits operational experience discovered by the others. By shifting the aggregation boundary from raw workloads to learned adaptations, EvolveNet keeps workloads local and allows multiple evolutionary searches to proceed concurrently with reduced serial depth. Because independently modified programs cannot be averaged like model parameters and may conflict when composed, EvolveNet introduces scope-typed, evidence-guided program aggregation. Across five settings spanning text-to-SQL, data-science coding, competitive programming, software engineering, and agentic workflows, EvolveNet improves the shared harness in all five, with the largest gains under heterogeneous workloads, and ablations attribute the improvement to composition of adaptations from different agents rather than to selecting among them.

</details>


### [155] [Stochastic Emulation using Generalized Stratified Sampling for Performance-Based Risk Optimization of Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05006)
*Isabela D. Rodrigues,Seymour M. J. Spence,Henrique M. Kroetz,André T. Beck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合广义分层采样（GSS）与随机多项式混沌展开（SPCE）的框架，用于性能基础风险优化（PBRO）中结构响应分布的准确估计，特别是尾部极端响应，并显著减少非线性模型评估次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPCE虽不需要固定输入条件下的重复非线性分析，但在准确表示结构响应分布尾部的极端响应方面存在局限性，需要改进以更好地捕捉极端响应。

Method: 提出GSS-SPCE框架：采用广义分层采样（GSS）按灾害强度将输入空间划分为层，改善极端响应表示；在各层内训练独立的SPCE模拟器；利用全概率公式重组各层条件超越概率以评估概率约束。

Result: 将框架应用于两层钢结构建筑屈曲约束支撑截面积的最优设计，结果表明该框架能准确估计结构响应分布（包括尾部区域），同时大幅减少PBRO所需的非线性模型评估次数。

Conclusion: GSS-SPCE框架有效解决了SPCE在尾部极端响应表示上的不足，为结构性能基础风险优化提供了计算高效且准确的方法。

Abstract: Metamodels are instrumental in reducing the computational burden associated with nested reliability analyses and optimization loops in Performance-Based Risk Optimization (PBRO) of structures under stochastic loads. In this context, stochastic emulators are particularly useful because they approximate response distributions while accounting for the intrinsic stochasticity of the simulator. Among these methods, Stochastic Polynomial Chaos Expansion (SPCE) is especially attractive because it does not require replications of nonlinear analyses at fixed input conditions. However, SPCE may present limitations in accurately representing extreme responses in the tails of structural response distributions. To address this limitation, this study proposes a framework that combines Generalized Stratified Sampling (GSS) with SPCE. The GSS scheme partitions the input space into strata according to the intensity of the hazard, improving the representation of extreme responses, while independent SPCE emulators are trained within each stratum. The conditional exceedance probabilities estimated in each stratum are then recombined using the total probability theorem to evaluate the probabilistic constraints. The proposed GSS-SPCE framework is applied to the optimal design of buckling-restrained brace cross-sectional areas in a two-story steel building. The objective is to minimize the initial construction cost while satisfying prescribed probabilistic performance constraints. Results show that the proposed framework accurately estimates structural response distributions, including their tail regions, while substantially reducing the number of nonlinear model evaluations required for PBRO.

</details>


### [156] [Canonical Joint Energy-Based Model on CIFAR-10: failure modes and practical indistinguishability of Predictor-Corrector and SGLD samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05025)
*Dmytro Knopov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文复现了标准 JEM 模型，系统对比了 SGLD 与 Predictor-Corrector (PC) 采样器在训练、生成和 OOD 检测三大协议上的表现，发现两者在实际性能上无显著差异，且 PC 在固定噪声下理论优势失效，同时记录了训练后期发散和 SVHN OOD 检测随运行波动两大失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 JEM 依赖 SGLD 训练，理论上有优势的 Predictor-Corrector (PC) 采样器未在标准模型上经过系统复现测试，需要验证 PC 在无退火噪声调度下是否保持理论优势。

Method: 在无归一化层的 WideResNet-28-10 上复现标准 JEM，进行两轮独立实验。对比三种协议：1) PC 替代 SGLD 贯穿约 130 个训练轮次；2) 冷启动生成 (FID)；3) 精炼式多 OOD 检测 (AUROC)。采用分层种子-图像 bootstrap 和种子级等价检验进行统计分析。

Result: 复现达到 92.88% 测试准确率和 buffer-FID 44.46 (标准值 92.9% 和 38.40)。发现两大失效模式：标准异常值缓冲机制导致的训练后期灾难性发散 (SGLD 和 PC 均出现)，以及依赖运行的 SVHN OOD 区分动态。PC 无方法级优势：推理时 AUROC 绝对差 <0.007，FID 差 <0.5；训练协议上宏平均 AUROC 差异 95% CI 包含零，两轮实验无法建立形式等价性。数据兼容等价性与微小方向性效应。固定噪声下 PC 预测步退化，理论保证不迁移至标准 JEM。

Conclusion: 在标准 JEM 设置下，PC 采样器未表现出相对于 SGLD 的实际优势，两者性能实用上不可区分。固定噪声调度下 PC 的理论优势因构造原因退化，且训练过程存在后期发散和 OOD 检测不稳定等鲁棒性问题。

Abstract: Joint Energy-Based Models (JEM) unify classification and generation within a single network and support out-of-distribution (OOD) detection. Canonical JEM training relies on stochastic gradient Langevin dynamics (SGLD); a theoretically motivated alternative, the Predictor-Corrector (PC) sampler, has not previously undergone a systematic replication test on the canonical model. We reproduce canonical JEM on WideResNet-28-10 without normalisation layers on two independent runs and test whether PC retains its theoretical advantage without an annealed noise schedule, across three protocols: PC replacing SGLD throughout the roughly 130 training epochs; cold-start generation (FID); and refinement-style multi-OOD detection (AUROC). The reconstruction reaches 92.88% test accuracy and buffer-FID 44.46 (canonical: 92.9% and 38.40). We document two failure modes: catastrophic late-training divergence via the canonical outlier-buffer mechanism (both SGLD runs and, with the same signature, both PC runs), and run-dependent SVHN OOD-discrimination dynamics. No method-level advantage of PC over SGLD is observed on any protocol: at inference the absolute AUROC difference stays below 0.007 across all ten checkpoint-OOD pairs and the FID difference below 0.5; on the training protocol a hierarchical seed-by-image bootstrap gives a 95% confidence interval on the macro-averaged AUROC difference that contains zero, while a seed-level equivalence test with two runs per method cannot establish formal equivalence. The data are consistent both with equivalence and with a small directional effect. This practical indistinguishability is theoretically expected: under fixed noise the PC predictor step degenerates by construction, so its guarantees do not transfer to canonical JEM.

</details>


### [157] [MultiPathFormer: Towards a Foundation Model for Multipath Wireless Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05076)
*Blessed Guda,Kayley Sze,Carlee Joe-Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MultiPathFormer 是一种基于多径传播的无线基础模型，采用自回归下一径预测预训练，结合环境检索增强生成和首径码本，在下游任务中超越现有信道基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无线基础模型基于信道张量进行掩码重构预训练，忽略了无线传播的物理特性，因此提出以多径传播为基本预训练对象，学习可复用的传播表示。

Method: 提出 MultiPathFormer，将每个收发链路表示为有序连续值径标记序列，采用自回归下一径预测预训练；引入环境 RAG 机制和首径码本，利用环境知识改进时延、功率等径统计估计。

Result: 在 27 个环境预训练后，迁移至未见用户并经场景微调，优于从头训练；下游任务中定位误差 5.57m，Top-3 波束准确率 0.914，LoS 分类准确率 0.994，信道估计 NMSE 0.561，均优于 SOTA 信道基础模型。

Conclusion: 基于径级别的预训练能够学习无线传播的可复用表示，为无线基础模型提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in machine learning have enabled training of wireless foundation models, which aim to support tasks such as channel estimation, beam prediction, and localization based on wireless signals. Existing wireless foundation models typically pretrain on channel tensors using masked reconstruction over subcarriers, antennas, or time but ignore the physical characteristics of wireless propagation. In this work, we propose to instead use multipath propagation as the fundamental pretraining object. We present MultiPathFormer, an autoregressive foundation model that represents each transmitter-receiver link as an ordered sequence of continuous-valued path tokens and pretrains with next-path prediction. We introduce an Environmental RAG (retrieval-augmented generation) mechanism and a first-path codebook on top of the transformer backbone, leveraging environment knowledge to improve path statistics estimation like delay and power by up to 59%. MultiPathFormer pretrained on 27 environments transfers to unseen users and, after scenario-specific fine-tuning, outperforms training the corresponding models from scratch in new environments. Across downstream tasks, it outperforms SOTA channel-based foundation models, achieving 5.57 m mean localization error, 0.914 top-3 beam accuracy, 0.994 line-of-sight classification accuracy, and 0.561 channel estimation NMSE. These results show that path-level pretraining can learn reusable representations of wireless propagation.

</details>


### [158] [Optimizing What Policies Learn From: Recoverability-aware Rollout Intervention Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05080)
*Zheyuan Zhang,Manqing Mao,Hong Wang,Zhuoer Wang,Samson Koelle,Jie Yuan,Yanjun Lin,James Feng,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Yanfang Ye,Wei Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为RAIL的框架，通过基于可恢复性的干预学习，在后训练大语言模型时自适应地生成更有信息量的rollout，解决了现有方法固定启发式且无法随策略演化调整的问题，在有限rollout预算下持续提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无评论家群组强化学习在后训练大语言模型时，对每个任务和轨迹状态分配相同数量的rollout，忽略了不同rollout提供的学习信号差异；近期自适应方法虽将rollout生成视为自适应决策，但存在两大局限：干预策略基于固定启发式无法随策略演化调整，且仅决定生成多少rollout而未显式控制干预位置和方式。因此需要一种能随策略演化持续学习、显式建模干预选择的方法。

Method: 提出Recoverability-Aware Intervention Learning (RAIL)框架，将干预选择建模为在线上下文多臂老虎机问题，通过影子到实时过程收集干预轨迹训练可恢复性控制器，使控制器能在底层策略演化过程中持续学习，根据每次干预产生的改进来学习如何生成rollout。

Result: 在多个设置下评估RAIL的有效性、自适应性、表达性和效率，结果显示RAIL在有限rollout预算下持续提升性能，表明可恢复性感知干预能生成更具信息量且冗余更少的rollout，为后训练提供更强学习信号。

Conclusion: RAIL提供了一种原则性的方法，通过学习基于可恢复性的干预策略，实现自适应、高效的rollout生成，显著改善后训练大语言模型的学习效果，且能随策略演化持续优化。

Abstract: Critic-free group-based reinforcement learning has become a scalable approach for post-training large language models. However, most existing methods allocate the same number of rollouts to every task and trajectory state, even though some rollouts provide much more useful learning signals than others. Recent work has started to treat rollout generation as an adaptive decision, but two important limitations remain. First, intervention strategies are often based on fixed heuristics and therefore cannot adjust as the policy changes during training. Second, these methods usually decide only how many rollouts to generate, without explicitly controlling where and how to intervene. To address these limitations, we propose Recoverability-Aware Intervention Learning (RAIL), a training-time framework that learns how to generate rollouts based on the improvement produced by each intervention. RAIL models intervention selection as an online contextual-bandit problem and trains a recoverability controller using intervention traces collected through a shadow-to-live procedure. This allows the controller to keep learning while the underlying policy evolves. We evaluate RAIL in terms of effectiveness, adaptivity, expressiveness, and efficiency. Across multiple settings, RAIL consistently improves performance under limited rollout budgets. These results show that recoverability-aware intervention provides a principled way to generate more informative and less redundant rollouts, leading to stronger learning signals during post-training.

</details>


### [159] [Learning When to Stop: Prefix-Optimal Dynamic Diffusion Policies for Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05084)
*Rohit Kumar Salla,Manoj Saravanan,Simon Stepputtis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Prefix-Optimal Generative Policies (POGP)，通过在去噪链上学习前缀价值函数，实现自适应早停和辅助训练目标，在 MuJoCo 任务上将去噪步数降低约 2.7 倍，同时性能提升约 3.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略在连续控制中表现强劲，但迭代去噪过程计算开销大；需根据动作难度动态调整去噪步数，以在保持性能的前提下降低计算成本。

Method: POGP 框架通过贝尔曼风格的递归在每个中间去噪步学习前缀价值函数，该函数既作为辅助训练目标鼓励中间输出成为高质量动作，又在测试时提供停止规则，当额外步骤不再带来显著改进时终止去噪。

Result: 在四个 MuJoCo 环境及 12 个基线对比中，POGP 将所需去噪迭代次数减少约 2.7 倍，保持近全任务性能；相比最先进的动态扩散基线，前缀训练还能使最终任务性能提升约 3.5%。

Conclusion: 监督中间去噪步骤不仅有助于自适应早停，还能作为辅助目标改进策略学习，前缀价值函数是实现这一双重收益的关键。

Abstract: Diffusion policies are a powerful policy class for continuous control, but their iterative denoising process creates a substantial computational bottleneck. Reducing this cost requires adapting the number of denoising steps to the difficulty of each action while preserving task performance. We introduce Prefix-Optimal Generative Policies (POGP), a framework that learns a prefix value function at every intermediate denoising step through a Bellman-style recursion over the denoising chain. The prefix value function serves two purposes: it provides an auxiliary training objective that encourages intermediate outputs to become high-quality actions, and it enables a test-time stopping rule that terminates denoising when additional steps are unlikely to produce meaningful improvement. Across four MuJoCo environments and comparisons with 12 baselines, POGP reduces the required number of denoising iterations by approximately 2.7-fold while retaining near-full task performance. Compared with state-of-the-art dynamic diffusion baselines, prefix training also improves final task performance by approximately 3.5%. These results indicate that supervising intermediate denoising steps is useful not only for adaptive early stopping, but also as an auxiliary objective that improves the learned policy.

</details>


### [160] [Capability-Gated Planning: Cost-to-Goal Discovery and the Limits of Myopic Experiment Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05085)
*Ahmed Hassoon,Mark Dredze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出自动化科学发现中短视规划的局限性，提出能力门控概念，证明短视规划器在需要构建性实验链时表现任意差，并提出CG-Plan规划器结合能力感知启发式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化科学发现系统依赖短视评分（如单位成本的期望信息增益或习得合理性分数），忽视了构建性实验的价值——它们获取认知能力（仪器、分析流程、模拟器等），其价值在于启用未来动作而非即时信息。当最低成本路径需要一系列此类构建时，有限展望的规划器无法为首个构建赋值，导致规划失败。

Method: 将目标导向发现形式化为信念空间中的随机最短路径问题，其中构建性实验改变下游动作图。证明对于任意展望深度d，存在实例使得每个短视信息最大化规划器具有无界近似比，且相关实例中永远无法达成目标。引入能力不可区分引理：在展望范围内，获取能力在观测上与为空动作付费无法区分。提出CG-Plan，一种增量重规划器，采用能力感知的成本到达启发式 h = h_cap + h_exp。

Result: 在受控测试床中，性能差距仅在门控条件下出现，对每个固定展望深度持续存在，且当近似假设来自数据一致提议器时产生。

Conclusion: 能力门控是科学发现规划中一个独特的可达性难度维度，区别于曲率（亚模性）和信息阶（自适应间隙）。CG-Plan通过能力感知启发式有效解决了这一问题。

Abstract: Systems that automate scientific discovery must repeatedly decide which experiment to run, which hypothesis to test, which tool to build, and when to stop. Many systems make these decisions by maximizing a myopic score such as expected information gain per unit cost or a learned plausibility score. We identify a structural limitation of this approach. Some actions are constructive: they acquire an epistemic capability (an instrument, assay, pipeline, simulator, or abstraction) whose value lies not in the information returned immediately but in the future actions it makes available. When the least-cost route to a confident answer requires a chain of such constructions, a planner that scores actions only by information obtainable within a bounded horizon cannot value the first construction: it yields no information within the horizon and is dominated by any measurement with positive information, however small. We formulate goal-directed discovery as a stochastic shortest-path problem in belief space in which constructive experiments change the downstream action graph, and prove that for every lookahead depth d there is an instance on which every myopic information-maximizing planner has an unbounded approximation ratio, and a related instance on which it never reaches the goal. The mechanism is a capability-indistinguishability lemma: within the horizon, acquiring a capability can be observationally indistinguishable from paying for a null action. This establishes capability gating as a reachability axis of difficulty distinct from curvature (submodularity) and information order (adaptivity gaps). We introduce CG-Plan, an incremental replanner with a capability-aware cost-to-go heuristic h = h_cap + h_exp. In a controlled testbed, the performance gap appears only under gating, persists for every fixed horizon, and arises when near-miss hypotheses come from a data-consistent proposer.

</details>


### [161] [MALT: Lightweight Curvature-Aware Muon via Diagonal Preconditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05088)
*Tongle Wu,Huanyu Dong,Ying Sun,Ziye Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MALT（轻量级双侧预条件增强的Muon）和MALTER（带自适应步长重缩放的MALT），通过引入轻量级对角预条件器解决Muon对曲率各向异性的敏感性，在GPT-2预训练中超越Muon且内存与时间开销几乎不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon虽通过Newton-Schulz正交化缓解梯度各向异性，但未显式考虑损失地形的曲率几何，仍对曲率各向异性敏感。

Method: MALT采用双侧对角预条件器近似捕捉曲率几何，对预条件动量进行Newton-Schulz正交化并映射回更新方向，用范数嫁接控制更新幅度；MALTER进一步引入自适应步长重缩放提升对随机梯度噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: 在GPT-2 Small/Medium/Large预训练实验中，MALT和MALTER均优于Muon，且内存占用和训练耗时几乎持平。

Conclusion: 提出的方法填补了Muon与曲率感知优化的空白，在随机非凸设定下提供收敛保证，为大模型预训练提供了更高效的优化器选择。

Abstract: Muon has recently emerged as a promising alternative to AdamW for language model pretraining by orthogonalizing momentum matrices using Newton-Schulz iterations. Although Muon mitigates gradient anisotropy, it does not explicitly account for the curvature geometry of the loss landscape and may therefore remain sensitive to curvature anisotropy. We bridge this gap by proposing MALT (Muon Augmented by Lightweight Two-sided Preconditioning), which uses lightweight diagonal preconditioners to reduce the sensitivity of Muon to curvature anisotropy. Specifically, MALT uses two-sided diagonal preconditioners with low memory and computational overhead to approximately capture the curvature geometry of the loss landscape. It orthogonalizes the preconditioned momentum using Newton-Schulz iterations and maps the result back to define the update direction, while norm grafting controls the update magnitude. To improve the robustness of MALT to stochastic gradient noise, we further propose MALTER (MALT with Adaptive stEpsize Rescaling). Convergence guarantees are provided for MALT in the stochastic non-convex setting. Experiments on GPT-2 Small, Medium, and Large pretraining show that the proposed methods outperform Muon while maintaining nearly the same memory footprint and wall-clock time.

</details>


### [162] [Multimodal Spatiotemporal Atmospheric Data Assimilation with Latent Flow-matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05103)
*Dibyajyoti Chakraborty,Romit Maulik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于潜在视频流匹配的大气数据同化统一方法，利用ERA5再分析数据训练先验，通过后验采样同化真实观测，实现了滤波、平滑等多种同化任务，并从稀疏观测直接生成全状态集合预报，性能可媲美最先进的观测到预报模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据同化依赖贝叶斯推断更新数值预报模型状态，但缺乏统一框架处理时序一致性和稀疏观测。本文旨在开发一种根本不同的统一方法，利用生成模型的连续轨迹特性自然传播观测与非观测时刻间的信息，简化滤波、平滑等任务并实现从稀疏观测到全状态集合预报的直接映射。

Method: 采用潜在视频流匹配训练先验模型（基于ERA5再分析的69个变量、8天窗口），利用后验采样同化NOAA综合全球探空档案和综合地面数据库的真实观测。先验生成的连续轨迹自然传播时序信息，通过改变观测帧位置实现滤波、平滑等不同同化任务，直接从稀疏观测生成全状态集合预报。

Result: 该方法在多种数据同化任务中表现优异：滤波和实验平滑性能具有竞争力；从稀疏观测直接生成的全状态集合预报，精度可媲美最先进的观测到预报模型；统一框架简化了不同同化任务的实现。

Conclusion: 潜在视频流匹配为大气数据同化提供了统一的生成式框架，通过连续时序先验和后验采样，有效解决了稀疏观测同化、时序信息传播和多任务统一的问题，为下一代数据同化系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Data assimilation (DA) uses Bayesian inference to update the state of a numerical forecast model with observed data. In this study, we propose a fundamentally different, unified approach to atmospheric data assimilation. We use latent video flow-matching to sample temporally consistent trajectories from a prior trained using ERA5 reanalysis (69 variables over an 8-day window). We also use posterior sampling to assimilate real observation sources, such as those from the NOAA Integrated Global Radiosonde Archive and the Integrated Surface Database. Because the prior generates a continuous trajectory, it naturally propagates information between observed and unobserved frames. Therefore, we can perform various DA tasks, such as filtering and smoothing, simply by changing the observed frames. Moreover, we generate full-state ensemble forecasts directly from sparse observations, achieving performance competitive with state-of-the-art observation-to-forecast models.

</details>


### [163] [BnBERT-iPET: Sparse Few-Shot Language Modeling for Bengali via Lottery Ticket Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05104)
*Sajib Hossain,Md Kamrus Samad,Anan Ghosh,Labib Imam Chowdhury,Nabeel Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出BnBERT-iPET，一种针对孟加拉语的稀疏少样本语言建模方法，通过迭代模式利用训练（iPET）和彩票假设剪枝实现90%稀疏度，仅保留初始模型10%的边，在孟加拉语下游任务上与大型最先进模型性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型预训练模型（如BERT）训练成本高昂、碳足迹大、难以在资源受限设备上部署，为孟加拉语等资源匮乏语言训练复杂模型造成障碍。神经网络中并非所有边同等重要，剪枝可减少内存占用、缩短训练时间、提高推理效率而不牺牲性能。

Method: 引入BnBERT-iPET模型，结合迭代模式利用训练（iPET）进行少样本学习，并应用彩票假设剪枝技术实现90%稀疏度，仅保留初始BERT模型10%的边。

Result: 剪枝后的BnBERT-iPET在孟加拉语标准基准数据集的下游任务上，与Bangla Electra、Indic-BERT、XLM-RoBERTa等最先进语言模型表现相当，证明轻量级少样本模型能与大型模型竞争。

Conclusion: 稀疏少样本语言建模方法结合剪枝技术，可为资源受限语言提供高效、高性能的解决方案，实现模型轻量化同时保持竞争力。

Abstract: Deep neural networks have shown impressive success in NLP tasks owing to their complex structure and huge number of edges. Achieving state-of-the-art performance in natural language processing with a large pre-trained model such as BERT is expensive and time-consuming, carries a large carbon footprint, and is difficult to realize on machines with minimal computational capability. This creates a barrier to training complex models for resource-constrained languages such as Bengali. However, in a complex neural model, not all edges are equally impactful, and the contributions of some of them can be neglected. Pruning promises to reduce the memory footprint of regular networks, shorten the training time of ever-growing networks, and increase inference efficiency without sacrificing comparable performance. In this work, we introduce BnBERT-iPET, a sparse few-shot language modeling approach for Bengali, and experimentally show that a lightweight few-shot-learned language model retaining only 10% of the edges of an initial model such as BERT can perform neck and neck with much larger models on challenging tasks for a resource-constrained language such as Bengali. By learning from few shots through iterative pattern exploiting training and achieving 90% sparsity with the Lottery Ticket Hypothesis pruning technique, our pruned BnBERT-iPET model proves to be a tough competitor to state-of-the-art language models such as Bangla Electra, Indic-BERT, and XLM-RoBERTa on downstream tasks over standard benchmark datasets of the Bengali language.

</details>


### [164] [Reward Structure Shapes the Interaction Between Episodic Exploration and Neural Memory in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05111)
*Jai Malegaonkar,Rohan Patil,Henrik I. Christensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究部分可观测强化学习中探索奖励与记忆架构的交互作用，通过受控实验发现二者是互补而非替代关系：探索奖励诱导智能体接触奖励状态，记忆将接触转化为回报。论文形式化定义了奖励稀疏性，区分结构稀疏性与潜在稀疏性，并验证了三种交互模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测强化学习中，智能体面临双重瓶颈：必须探索以遇到奖励状态，并将经验保留在记忆中以优化策略。现有工作孤立地评估探索奖励和记忆架构，未测量二者的交互；标准稀疏奖励概念混淆了时间信号密度与奖励实际监督的内容。

Method: 设计受控实验，结合情节式探索奖励与多种神经记忆架构，在三个记忆内容获取方式不同的环境中测试。通过控制奖励操作验证交互模式是否跟踪奖励结构而非密度。引入观测锚定奖励机器形式化奖励稀疏性，区分结构稀疏性和潜在稀疏性。

Result: 相同的奖励信号产生三种截然不同的交互模式：1) 在记忆内容需主动发现并无监督保留的环境中，奖励放大架构容量差异；2) 在内容一旦寻找即为单一奖励监督线索的环境中，奖励使架构达到共享上限；3) 在观测流纯粹调度的环境中，奖励无效。密集奖励仅当直接监督所需潜在记忆时才中和奖励；对探索动作施加小惩罚导致次优平稳状态，奖励可解决。形式化词汇按任务暴露的保留负担组织三种模式。

Conclusion: 探索与记忆是互补而非替代关系：探索奖励诱导暴露，只有记忆能将暴露转化为回报。奖励稀疏性应按结构稀疏性（自动机无需任务所需历史即可复现回报）与潜在稀疏性（单步奖励错误定价局部探索动作）区分。

Abstract: In partially observable reinforcement learning, agents face a dual bottleneck: they must explore to encounter rewarding states and retain that experience in memory to optimize their policies. Exploration bonuses and memory architectures are traditionally evaluated in isolation, leaving their interaction unmeasured, and standard notions of sparse reward conflate temporal signal density with what the reward actually supervises. We present a controlled study crossing episodic exploration bonuses with diverse neural memory architectures across three environments that vary how the content of memory is acquired. An identical bonus signal yields three distinct interaction patterns: it amplifies architectural capacity differences where memory content must be actively discovered and retained unsupervised; equalizes architectures to a shared ceiling where the content, once sought out, is a single reward-supervised cue; and is null where the observation stream is purely scheduled. Controlled reward manipulations verify that these patterns track reward structure rather than density: a dense reward neutralizes a bonus only if it directly supervises the required latent memory, and a small avoidable penalty on exploratory actions (leaving the optimum unchanged) induces policy convergence to suboptimal stationary states, which either bonus resolves. We then formalize reward sparsity with observation-anchored reward machines, separating structural sparsity (an automaton reproduces the return without the task-required history) from potential sparsity (the one-step reward misprices local exploratory actions); the resulting vocabulary organizes the three regimes by the retention burden each task exposes. Together, these results show exploration and memory are complements, not substitutes: a bonus induces exposure, and only memory converts exposure into return.

</details>


### [165] [DASyR-LLM: Domain-Aware Symbolic Regression with LLMs for Kinetic Model Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05120)
*Roberto Aliaga Medina,Paulina Quintanilla,Antonio del Rio Chanona*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于大语言模型(LLM)指导的符号回归(SR)框架，用于自动化动力学模型发现。LLM在每次迭代中扮演两个角色：对最佳SR候选模型进行物理化学定性评价，以及基于SR生成的模型和化学知识提出新的候选速率表达式。在四个不同复杂度的计算案例研究中，该框架将识别真实模型所需的迭代次数减少了41.7%-79.3%，且在超过一半的引导运行中直接提出了正确的模型结构。预测性能与纯SR方法相当(R^2>0.98)。消融研究表明SR组件和LLM规模均有贡献，且较小规模的LLM也能基本保持发现效率。这表明LLM能有效将领域知识注入科学模型发现，为全自动化、领域感知的动力学建模流程铺平道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学模型发现是化学工程的核心挑战，准确的速率表达式对于理解和控制化学及生物过程至关重要。符号回归(SR)作为一种强大的数据驱动方法，能识别可解释的动力学模型，但通常缺乏领域知识，往往会探索物理化学上不可信的模型。大语言模型(LLM)为将领域专业知识注入搜索过程提供了有前景的途径。

Method: 引入一个LLM指导的SR框架，将LLM模块嵌入迭代SR算法中以实现自动化动力学模型发现。LLM在每次迭代中扮演两个角色：(1)对最佳SR候选模型进行定性物理化学评价；(2)基于SR生成的模型和嵌入的化学知识提出新的候选速率表达式。

Result: 在四个复杂度递增的体外案例研究(涵盖异相催化和生物过程系统)中评估。结果显示，LLM指导框架将识别真实模型所需的迭代次数比最先进的SR框架减少了41.7%-79.3%，且在超过一半的引导运行中LLM直接提出了正确的模型结构。在实际场景中，每次迭代通常需要新的湿实验，这意味着实验工作量大幅减少。在独立验证集上的预测性能两种方法相当，所有案例研究中R^2>0.98。消融研究表明SR组件和LLM规模均对性能有贡献，且缩小规模的LLM基本保持了发现效率。

Conclusion: 研究表明LLM能有效将领域知识注入科学模型发现，为全自动化、领域感知的动力学建模流程铺平了道路。

Abstract: Kinetic model discovery is a central challenge in chemical engineering, as accurate rate expressions are essential for understanding and controlling chemical and biological processes. Symbolic regression (SR) has emerged as a powerful data-driven approach for identifying interpretable kinetic models, but usually operates without domain knowledge, often exploring physicochemically implausible models. Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising avenue for injecting domain expertise into this search. Here, we introduce an LLM-guided SR framework, embedding an LLM module within an iterative SR algorithm for automated kinetic model discovery. The LLM performs two roles at each iteration: (1) a qualitative physicochemical critique of the best SR candidates, and (2) the proposal of new candidate rate expressions guided by the SR-generated models and embedded chemical knowledge. Our framework is evaluated on four in silico case studies of increasing complexity, spanning heterogeneous catalysis and bioprocess systems. Results show the LLM-guided framework reduces iterations to identify the ground-truth model by $41.7-79.3\%$ versus a state-of-the-art SR framework, with the LLM directly proposing the correct model structure in over half of the guided runs. In practical settings, where each iteration typically requires a new wet-lab experiment, this translates into a substantial reduction in experimental effort. Predictive performance on an independent validation set is equivalent between both approaches, with $R^2>0.98$ in all case studies. Ablation studies indicate that both the SR component and the LLM scale contribute to this performance, with a reduced-size LLM largely retaining discovery efficiency. These findings demonstrate that LLMs can effectively inject domain knowledge into scientific model discovery, paving the way toward fully automated, domain-aware kinetic modelling pipelines.

</details>


### [166] [SSTQ:Privacy-Preserving Vector Quantization via Subsampled Stochastic TurboQuant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05127)
*Adel Javanmard,David P. Woodruff,Vahab Mirrokni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Subsampled Stochastic TurboQuant (SSTQ) 框架，结合过完备等范数紧框架、坐标子采样和隐私感知一维量化，实现局部差分隐私下的最优均方误差缩放，通信成本仅为 ⌈log₂ N⌉ + b 位/客户端，并将码本相关的 MSE 缩放从 O(4^b) 降至 O(2^b)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的向量量化方法（如 vqSGD）虽利用高维几何构造，但带来不利的维度相关方差，难以在低通信成本下实现局部差分隐私。

Method: SSTQ 框架包含两个变体：Flat Randomized Response 和 Metric-Aware Laplace（适合高码本位宽）。核心技术包括过完备等范数紧框架、坐标子采样、隐私感知一维量化，以及代理隐私感知码本目标。

Result: 理论上证明 SSTQ 达到最优 MSE 缩放，通信比特为 ⌈log₂ N⌉ + b（N=Θ(d)）；码本相关 MSE 缩放从 O(4^b) 降为 O(2^b)。在 CIFAR-10 和 Fashion-MNIST 联邦学习任务上实验验证了其效用和通信效率优势。

Conclusion: SSTQ 通过创新的框架设计和码本优化，在保证局部差分隐私的同时实现了低通信开销下的最优收敛性能，为分布式优化中的隐私保护提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Achieving local differential privacy in distributed optimization while maintaining low communication cost remains challenging. Existing vector quantization methods, such as vqSGD, use high-dimensional geometric constructions but incur unfavorable dimension-dependent variance. In this work, we propose Subsampled Stochastic TurboQuant (SSTQ), a framework that combines overcomplete equal-norm tight frames, coordinate subsampling, and privacy-aware one-dimensional quantization. SSTQ includes two variants: a Flat Randomized Response version and a Metric-Aware Laplace version, the latter being better suited to higher codebook bit-width regimes. We show that SSTQ achieves optimal mean squared error scaling while using only $\lceil \log_2 N \rceil + b$ bits per client, where $N = Θ(d)$ is the frame size. We also derive a surrogate privacy-aware codebook objective that reduces the codebook-dependent MSE scaling from $O(4^b)$ to $O(2^b)$. Finally, we empirically evaluate SSTQ against established baselines on federated learning tasks using CIFAR-10 and Fashion-MNIST, demonstrating favorable utility and communication efficiency.

</details>


### [167] [The Loss Does Not See the Basis, but Adam Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05136)
*Devender Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了分解模型 $W = UV^⊤$ 上不同优化器的隐式偏置，揭示了规范对称性与优化器规范等变性的关键作用。梯度下降、动量法、共享标量 Adam、Muon 和 Shampoo 满足规范等变性，能继承梯度流的低秩偏置；而 Adam、RMSProp 等坐标级方法不满足，导致无法恢复低秩解。通过理论分析（结构定理、迁移定理）和实验（欠定矩阵感知、Transformer、高光谱数据），证明了各向异性是导致偏置差异的原因，且基础选择决定了优化器选择的插值解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究为何在相同小初始化下，梯度下降在分解模型上隐式偏向低秩解，而 Adam 却不偏向。作者将差异归因于损失函数的规范对称性（在 $(U, V) ↦ (UQ, VQ)$ 下不变），并提出优化器必须具备规范等变性才能传递梯度流的低秩机制。

Method: 1. 理论分析：定义规范等变性，给出无记忆等变更新规则的结构定理（恰为 Gram 矩阵决定的左预条件子），以及将梯度流路径性质迁移到公共标量流的迁移定理。2. 实证研究：在欠定矩阵感知任务上对比九种更新规则的恢复误差；构造从坐标级到共享标量预条件的单参数族，单调恢复偏置；提出“谱调度”调和 Muon 的矛盾报告；在 Transformer 中考察 Adam 与等变优化器在规范等价初始化下的分离；在两个高光谱数据集上比较泛化误差与有效秩。

Result: 1. 规范等变优化器：梯度下降、动量、共享标量 Adam、Muon、Shampoo；非等变：Adam、RMSProp 等坐标级方法。2. 单参数族实验证实各向异性是偏置差异的单一原因。3. Muon 的谱调度：等速率更新能精确恢复低秩目标，但谱尾增长时优势消失。4. Transformer 实验：Adam 在第一步就分离规范等价初始化，最终各头不变量 $W_Q^⊤ W_K$ 相对 Frobenius 距离相差 56%，无法通过头内旋转消除。5. 高光谱数据：在相同训练损失下，梯度下降在最低采样密度下将保留误差降低 43-44%，且有效秩更低。

Conclusion: 基础选择不是调优细节，而是决定优化器选择哪个插值解的根本决策。规范等变性是优化器继承梯度流低秩隐式偏置的必要条件（非充分），坐标级预条件破坏该对称性，导致无法恢复低秩真值。

Abstract: Gradient descent on a factored model $W = UV^\top$ is implicitly biased toward low-rank solutions, while Adam, starting from the same small initialization, is not. We trace the difference to the gauge symmetry of the loss, its invariance under $(U, V) \mapsto (UQ, VQ)$. Gradient flow's low-rank mechanism is available to an optimizer only if that optimizer is gauge-equivariant, a condition necessary for the transfer but not sufficient for low-rank recovery. Gradient descent, momentum, "shared-scalar" Adam, Muon, and Shampoo satisfy it. Adam, RMSProp, and the other coordinate-wise methods do not. A structure theorem characterizes the memoryless equivariant rules as exactly the Gram-determined left preconditioners, and a transfer theorem carries gradient flow's pathwise properties to common-scalar flows. We then sort nine update rules on underdetermined matrix sensing by recovery error against the planted ground truth. A one-parameter family from coordinate-wise to shared-scalar preconditioning restores the bias monotonically, isolating anisotropy as the cause. A "spectral schedule" reconciles two opposing reports about Muon: equal-rate updates recover exactly low-rank targets but lose their edge as the spectral tail grows. In transformers, Adam separates two gauge-equivalent initializations at the first step, where the equivariant optimizers stay at float precision, and ends with the per-head invariants $W_Q^\top W_K$ 56% apart in relative Frobenius distance, a gap no per-head rotation can close. On two hyperspectral datasets at matched training loss, gradient descent cuts held-out error by 43-44% at the lowest sampling density, and at lower effective rank. Basis choice is therefore not a tuning detail but a decision about which interpolant the optimizer selects.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [168] [Monte Carlo Tree Search for Table-to-Multimodal Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04071)
*Teng Lin,Zhiyang Zhang,Yuyu Luo,Nan Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MCTS-Report框架，利用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）驱动多模态表格到报告的生成，将生成过程分解为原子动作并通过多维奖励函数联合优化事实准确性、图表质量、文图对齐和结构完整性，在MMRBench基准上显著优于基线，整体得分77.9。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法采用固定线性流水线且子任务孤立处理，难以联合优化事实准确性、视觉质量和叙事连贯性，亟需一种能够进行渐进式构建与联合推理的框架。

Method: 提出MCTS-Report框架：将报告生成建模为结构化搜索空间上的渐进构建过程，分解为章节规划、可视化任务识别、图表生成、洞察组织、叙事润色等原子动作，每步由LLM基于当前状态进行动态推理；在MCTS节点中存储推理轨迹以实现上下文感知；设计多维奖励函数（SQL数值一致性、图表质量、图文对齐、结构完整性、多样性惩罚、前置条件剪枝）引导搜索。

Result: 构建MMRBench基准（六领域真实表格、专家修订报告结构、可验证关键洞察）。实验显示MCTS-Report在结构完整性、数值准确性、图文对齐、洞察新颖性上显著超越强基线，整体得分达77.9。

Conclusion: MCTS-Report通过MCTS驱动的渐进式构建与多维奖励联合优化，有效解决了多模态表格报告生成中的事实性、视觉质量与叙事连贯性难以平衡的问题，为数据智能领域的自动化报告生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Automatically generating professional multimodal reports comprising both textual analysis and visual charts from structured tabular data is a critical challenge in data intelligence. Existing methods suffer from fixed linear pipelines and isolated subtask processing, which hinder joint optimization of factual accuracy, visual quality, and narrative coherence. To address these issues, this paper proposes MCTS-Report, a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS)-driven framework that formulates multimodal table-to-report generation as a progressive construction process over a structured search space. The core idea is to decompose report generation into atomic actions, including chapter planning, visualization task identification, chart generation, insight organization, and narrative refinement, each executed by an LLM based on dynamic reasoning conditioned on the current report state. We use an LLM to generate step-by-step reasoning and actions during MCTS, storing the reasoning trajectory in each node for context-aware, coherent report construction. To guide the search, we design a multi-dimensional reward function that jointly evaluates numerical fact consistency (via SQL), chart quality, chart-text alignment, and structural completeness, while incorporating a diversity penalty to suppress repeated charts and a precondition check to prune invalid actions. We also construct MMRBench, a comprehensive benchmark comprising real-world tables from six domains, paired with expert-refined reference report structures and verifiable key insights. Experiments on MMRBench demonstrate that MCTS-Report significantly outperforms strong baselines across structural completeness, numerical accuracy, chart-text alignment, and insight novelty, achieving a 77.9 overall score.

</details>


### [169] [SafeCommit: Certifying When Memory-Grounded Agents May Safely Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04289)
*Mayur Akewar,Ravi Ranjan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SafeCommit，一个用于长视野智能体在记忆不确定性下安全决策的风险控制层。它通过构建校准的潜在世界集合，仅在一致性动作证书证明该动作在所有保留世界中均安全时才允许执行具有外部副作用的动作，否则选择低副作用探针或保守回退。理论上在校准世界覆盖下，不安全认证提交的概率不超过目标水平 α；实验通过无依赖模拟器验证了安全性与效用的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野智能体越来越多地使用持久化记忆和工具执行具有外部副作用的动作。核心失效模式是过早承诺：智能体在解决记忆是否陈旧、冲突、不完整或损坏之前就采取行动。需要一种形式化的方法来决定何时证据足以安全地执行动作。

Method: 引入 SafeCommit 风险控制层，位于智能体推理与外部执行之间。该层从记忆、观察、工具输出、来源和策略约束构建校准的合理潜在世界集合。仅当一致性动作证书表明动作在每个保留世界中均安全时，才允许执行副作用动作；否则选择针对阻塞认证的世界的低副作用探针，或返回保守回退。理论分析给出在校准世界覆盖下的安全概率界，并在不完美世界提议下分离校准误差与表示误差。提供无依赖的受控模拟器复现结果。

Result: 在校准世界覆盖假设下，不安全认证提交的概率至多为目标风险水平 α；当世界提议不完美时，界限分离了校准误差和表示误差。实验展示了安全性与效用之间的权衡，且所有结果可通过单命令复现。

Conclusion: SafeCommit 提供了一种具体的方法，不仅决定智能体应该做什么，还决定何时可用证据足以安全地执行动作，为长视野智能体在记忆不确定性下的安全承诺提供了理论保障和实用框架。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents increasingly use persistent memory and tools to take actions with external side effects. A central failure mode is premature commitment: an agent acts before resolving whether its memory grounding is stale, conflicting, incomplete, or corrupted. We formalize this problem as safe commitment under memory uncertainty and introduce SafeCommit, a risk controlled layer between agent reasoning and external execution. The layer constructs a calibrated set of plausible latent worlds from memory, observations, tool outputs, provenance, and policy constraints. It permits a side effectful action only when a conformal action certificate shows that the action is safe in every retained world. Otherwise, it selects a low-side-effect probe that targets the worlds blocking certification, or returns a conservative fallback. Under calibrated world coverage, the probability of an unsafe certified commit is at most the target level α; with imperfect world proposal, the bound separates calibration and representation error. A dependency-free controlled simulator illustrates the safety-utility tradeoff and reproduces all reported results with one command. The goal is to offer a concrete approach for deciding not only what an agent should do, but when the available evidence is sufficient to safely do it.

</details>


### [170] [NeuMoSync: End-to-End Neuromodulatory Control for Plasticity and Adaptability in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04358)
*Seyed Roozbeh Razavi Rohani,Khashayar Khajavi,Wesley Chung,Mandana Samiei,Mo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 NeuMoSync，一种受大脑神经调节机制启发的持续学习架构，通过为每个神经元引入可学习特征向量和高层调节模块，动态调节激活动态和突触可塑性，在多个持续学习基准上显著提升前向/后向适应性能并保持可塑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习要求模型按序学习任务，但深度神经网络常遭遇可塑性丧失和知识迁移差的问题，限制了长期适应能力。受大脑全局神经调节机制启发，作者旨在将全局协调机制引入深度学习，增强模型的适应性和可塑性。

Method: NeuMoSync 扩展标准神经网络：为每个神经元分配可学习特征向量以追踪网络历史上下文；引入高层抽象模块，根据当前输入和网络演化状态合成神经元特异性调节信号，自适应调控激活动态和突触可塑性。

Result: 在记忆、概念漂移、类增量、域增量等多样 CL 基准上，NeuMoSync 表现出强的可塑性保持，且前向和后向适应均优于现有方法。消融实验验证各组件必要性，学习到的调节信号展现出跨任务的可解释协调模式。

Conclusion: 将全局协调机制集成到深度学习系统中，能有效推动鲁棒、自适应的持续学习发展。代码已开源。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) requires models to learn tasks sequentially, yet deep neural networks often suffer from plasticity loss and poor knowledge transfer, which can impede their long-term adaptability. Drawing high-level inspiration from global neuromodulatory mechanisms in the brain, we introduce Neuromodulation and Synchronization (NeuMoSync), a novel architecture that integrates dynamic, neuron-specific modulation into deep neural networks to enhance their adaptability and plasticity. NeuMoSync extends standard neural network architectures with learnable feature vectors for each neuron that track network-wide historical context and with a module operating at a higher level of abstraction. This module synthesizes neuron-specific signals, conditioned on both current inputs and the network's evolving state, to adaptively regulate activation dynamics and synaptic plasticity. Evaluated on diverse CL benchmarks, including memorization (Random Label CIFAR-10 and Random Label MNIST), concept drift (Shuffle CIFAR-10 and Shuffle Mini-ImageNet), class-incremental learning (Class Split ImageNet and Class Split CIFAR-100), and domain-incremental learning (Permuted MNIST), NeuMoSync demonstrates strong performance in retaining plasticity and achieves improvements in both forward and backward adaptation compared with existing methods. Ablation studies validate the necessity of each component, while analysis of the learned modulatory signals reveals interpretable coordination patterns across tasks. Our work underscores the potential of integrating global coordination mechanisms into deep learning systems to advance robust, adaptive continual learning. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/RoozbehRazavi/NeuMoSync.

</details>


### [171] [Improving Auto-Design of Neural PDE Solvers with a Domain-Specific Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04384)
*Shengxin Kong,Liwen Xu,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ADSL-PDE 提出了一种结构化搜索状态表示，用于神经 PDE 求解器的自动设计，使进化搜索在设计决策层面而非代码层面进行，在前十次迭代中将搜索效率和优化稳定性提升 52% 以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经 PDE 求解器自动设计本质是搜索空间表示问题。在无约束 Python 程序空间中，合法求解器极其稀疏，直接代码生成会让 LLM 耗费大量搜索容量在实现失败上，而非求解器质量推理。

Method: ADSL-PDE 引入位于求解器概念与可执行代码之间的结构化搜索状态，显式表示架构、物理约束、目标、采样、优化等功能性决策，并屏蔽底层实现细节。确定性编译器将每个合法搜索状态映射为可执行求解器。进化代理在此表示上迭代提出、评估、细化搜索状态，利用经验反馈引导搜索。

Result: 在多个 PDE 基准上，ADSL-PDE 同时提升了搜索效率与优化稳定性，前十次进化迭代即实现 52% 以上的性能提升。

Conclusion: 有效的 LLM 驱动自动设计不仅需要更强的推理能力，更需要一种能将探索集中在有效且关键决策上的搜索表示。

Abstract: Neural PDE solver auto-design is fundamentally a search-space representation problem. In the space of unrestricted Python programs, valid solvers form an extremely sparse subset: most candidate programs are syntactically incorrect, semantically incompatible, or numerically unstable. Direct code generation therefore forces an LLM to spend most of its search capacity navigating implementation failures rather than reasoning about solver quality. ADSL-PDE addresses this challenge by introducing a structured search state between solver concepts and executable code. It represents the functional decisions that determine a neural PDE solver (architecture, physical constraints, objectives, sampling, and optimization) while abstracting away low-level implementation details. A deterministic compiler maps each valid search state to an executable solver. In effect, ADSL-PDE reshapes the search space: it removes large regions of invalid programs, increases the density of meaningful candidates, and preserves the compositional freedom needed to discover previously unseen designs. Solver evolution can thus operate over design decisions rather than code artifacts. Built on this representation, our evolutionary agent iteratively proposes, evaluates, and refines solver search states using empirical feedback. Across multiple PDE benchmarks, ADSL-PDE improves both search efficiency and optimization stability, achieving an improvement of more than 52% within the first ten evolution iterations. These results suggest a broader principle for LLM-driven auto-design: effective agents do not merely require stronger reasoning, but rather a search representation that concentrates exploration on valid and consequential decisions.

</details>


### [172] [Leak-Resistant Unlearning: A New Benchmark for Evaluating Multi-Hop Reasoning Consistency and Recovery Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04519)
*Haoting Qian,Qingjie Zhang,Zhicong Huang,Cheng Hong,Han Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个新的基准测试框架，用于评估大语言模型（LLM）在多跳推理路径和恢复攻击下的知识遗忘鲁棒性，发现现有遗忘方法均存在脆弱性，并探讨了遗忘质量、鲁棒性与模型效用之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的机器遗忘基准主要包含单跳问题和有限的多跳问题，存在两大挑战：知识非孤立性导致多跳推理路径可能引发知识泄露，且遗忘过程可能脆弱，经过轻量级后遗忘适应等恢复攻击可部分恢复已遗忘知识，静态评估不足。

Method: 引入名为“unlearning”的新基准，在3个模型、6种遗忘方法和2个精心策划的数据集上进行实验，系统评估多跳推理路径和恢复攻击下的遗忘效果。

Result: 实验结果表明，现有遗忘方法均易受多跳推理路径和恢复攻击影响，遗忘知识存在泄露和可恢复风险。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了LLM遗忘在鲁棒性上的根本局限，强调需综合考量遗忘质量、鲁棒性与模型效用的权衡，为未来开发更可靠的遗忘方法提供了评估依据。

Abstract: Benchmarking machine unlearning methods is critical to understand whether sensitive knowledge is removed from large language models (LLMs) or not. Current unlearning benchmarks include mainly single-hop questions and a narrow set of multi-hop questions. Although effective, they still face two challenges. (1) Knowledge is not isolated, whereby diverse multi-hop reasoning paths can potentially induce knowledge leakage than normal queries. (2) Unlearning may be fragile: unlearned knowledge can be partially recovered through recovery attacks such as lightweight post-unlearning adaptation, making static evaluation insufficient. Therefore, in this paper, we introduce \unlearning as a novel benchmark to understand robust LLM knowledge removal across diverse reasoning paths and recovery attacks. We experiment with this benchmark on 3 models, 6 unlearning methods, and 2 carefully curated datasets. Results show that existing methods are vulnerable to multi-hop reasoning paths and recovery attacks. We further explore the trade-off among forget quality, robustness, and model utility for LLM unlearning.

</details>


### [173] [What Is a Skill Worth? Structure-Aware Shapley Valuation of Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04562)
*Tao Li,Junfeng Liu,Qinghua Zhao,Yifan Li,Lei Wang,Bo Shao,Xuejun Liu,Linjun Shou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出SkillSV框架，用于评估智能体技能内部结构单元的价值，解决技能单元结构化、依赖关系和上下文成本问题，实现忠实归因、安全剪枝和压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自动化反馈循环优化智能体技能，产生的长结构化工件内部价值不清。现有数据或提示片段估值方法不适用，因为技能单元具有结构依赖、层级归属、触发行为和消耗上下文等特性，需要结构感知的估值方法。

Method: 提出SkillSV，一种结构感知的Shapley风格框架。将技能编译为单元、依赖和层级，仅评估有效反事实技能；采用配对删除和长度中性填充分离内容价值与上下文成本；使用滚动预算估计器处理噪声智能体评估。

Result: 在四个智能体基准上验证，SkillSV能恢复单元交互、保持总体技能提升、指导安全剪枝和压缩，展现忠实性、可行动性和可解释性。

Conclusion: SkillSV为结构化技能单元提供了有效的估值框架，解决了技能内部归因难题，支持技能优化和压缩，推动智能体技能的可解释和高效开发。

Abstract: Agent skills are increasingly optimized by automated feedback loops, producing long structured artifacts whose internal value remains unclear. We study skill valuation: assigning credit to the internal units of a fixed skill, such as rules, examples, scripts, and heuristics, under a fixed agent and held-out task distribution. Skill valuation differs from data or prompt-span valuation because skill units are structured: they may depend on other units, belong to a document hierarchy, trigger agent behavior, and consume limited prompt context. We introduce SkillSV, a structure-aware Shapley-style framework for skill valuation. SkillSV compiles a skill into units, dependencies, and hierarchy, so that only valid counterfactual skills are evaluated. It uses paired deletion and length-neutral padding to separate content value from context cost, and estimates the resulting values with a rollout-budgeted estimator for noisy agent evaluations. On four agentic benchmarks, we assess the faithfulness, actionability, and explanation of SkillSV: it recovers unit interactions, preserves aggregate skill lift, and guides safe pruning and compression.

</details>


### [174] [Joint UAV Flight and Opportunistic Routing under Reinforcement Learning for Delay-Tolerant Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04590)
*Xiao Wang,Shun-Ren Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JUROR框架，联合优化分散式机会路由和可控UAV飞行，解决DTN中稀疏连接、有限缓冲和TTL导致的性能下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DTN中存储-携带-转发通信因间歇性接触、有限缓冲和消息TTL限制，导致稀疏投递和拥塞，严重降低端到端性能。

Method: 将问题建模为因子化部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程，采用中心化训练分散式执行（CTDE）架构，基于PPO框架设计JUROR，结合分散式演员基于局部观测行动、训练时评论家使用全局统计，以及可选的多时域热点预测器提供辅助监督。

Result: 在四种流量模式下的仿真实验中，JUROR优于PRoPHET和MaxProp基线，同时保持接触受限的分散式执行能力。

Conclusion: JUROR通过联合优化UAV飞行和机会路由，有效提升了DTN中的消息投递性能，为稀疏连接环境下的通信提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: The growing deployment of delay-tolerant networks (DTNs) has made store-carry-forward (SCF) communication indispensable under sparse connectivity.
  However, intermittent contacts, finite buffers, and limited message time-to-live (TTL) often give rise to sparse delivery and congestion, leading to substantial end-to-end performance degradation.
  To address this challenge, this study explores the joint optimization of decentralized opportunistic routing and controllable unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) flight, aiming to enlarge future contacts through discrete UAV headings while enabling per-node replication under contact-limited observations.
  Building upon this architecture, we study cooperative factored routing--UAV control under centralized training and decentralized execution (CTDE) and propose JUROR (Joint UAV flight and Opportunistic Routing, based on the proximal policy optimization (PPO) framework.
  In our design, we first cast the problem as a factored partially observable Markov decision process with sequential motion--routing coupling and a per-step team reward; subsequently, decentralized actors act on local observations while a training-time critic uses global statistics, and an optional multi-horizon hotspot predictor provides auxiliary supervision.
  Simulation results over four traffic modes demonstrate effective gains over PRoPHET and MaxProp, while retaining contact-limited decentralized execution.

</details>


### [175] [Agreement Before Diversity: Verification-First Complementarity for Heterogeneous Language-Model Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04618)
*Ruitong Li,Binjie Guo,Aisheng Mo,Guowei Su,Jie Li,Ru Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Agreement-Before-Diversity (ABD) 方法，通过锚定答案的验证机制决定是否保留或替换为异构合成答案，证明了两个精确恒等式量化准确率差距，在 LiveCodeBench-v6 和 GPQA-Diamond 上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构语言模型集成扩展了候选响应空间，但缺乏原则性标准判断何时应用新生成答案替换已支持答案。需要将候选余量与替换权威解耦，使替换权威成为显式、可审计的对象。

Method: 提出 Agreement-Before-Diversity (ABD) 冻结、无标签决策规则：若两个额外受信样本在固定等价关系下佐证锚定答案，则保留；否则用异构合成替换。证明两个精确恒等式：一、相对于无条件合成的准确率差距由一致覆盖率和锚定答案在受保护子集上的优势共同决定；二、相对于从不合成的差距反映授权恢复与授权破坏的对比。无需假设独立性或校准置信度，推理期望调用次数约为 8 - 5 * coverage。

Result: 在盲测、精确 ID 评估下，ABD 在完整 LiveCodeBench-v6 达到 59.43%（Single9 为 52.57%，HAC 为 52.00%，n=175）；在未触动的 GPQA-Diamond 分割上达到 75.00%（两对照均为 72.78%，n=180）。差异定位到可枚举受保护层：LiveCodeBench 上 3 个受保护案例中无不一致项，覆盖率将门控贡献先验限制在 1.71 分；GPQA-Diamond 上 132 个案例中 13 与 8 不一致；冻结锚定扰动下 71 个案例中 12 与 0 不一致。

Conclusion: 多样性提供潜力，验证结构提供权威。ABD 通过显式门控机制在无需标签或校准置信度的情况下，有效平衡了保留可靠答案与引入多样性合成的权衡，实现了可解释且性能优越的集成决策。

Abstract: Heterogeneous language-model ensembles expand the space of candidate responses, yet they lack a principled criterion for when a newly generated answer should supersede an already supported one. We decouple candidate headroom from replacement authority, rendering the latter as an explicit, auditable object. Our proposed method, Agreement-Before-Diversity (ABD), is a frozen, label-free decision rule: an anchor answer is retained if two additional trusted samples corroborate it under a fixed equivalence relation; otherwise, it is replaced by a heterogeneous synthesis. For this gating mechanism, we prove two exact identities. The first shows that the accuracy gap relative to unconditional synthesis is determined jointly by the agreement coverage and the anchor's advantage on the protected subset. The second shows that the gap relative to never synthesizing reflects a contrast between authorized recovery and authorized destruction. Neither identity assumes independence or calibrated confidence, and the expected inference cost is approximately eight minus five times the coverage in number of calls. Under blind, exact-ID evaluation, ABD achieves 59.43% on the complete LiveCodeBench-v6 (vs. 52.57% for Single9 and 52.00% for HAC; n = 175) and 75.00% on an untouched GPQA-Diamond split (both controls at 72.78%; n = 180). Furthermore, these identities localize every aggregate difference to an enumerable protected stratum: no discordant items occur among the 3 protected cases on LiveCodeBench, where coverage bounds the gate's contribution to 1.71 points a priori; 13 versus 8 discordant cases among 132 on GPQA-Diamond; and 12 versus 0 among 71 under a frozen anchor perturbation. Diversity supplies potential; verification structure supplies authority.

</details>


### [176] [A/B Agent: A Self-Evolving Agent for Strategy Iteration in Industrial A/B Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04625)
*Zhuohang Jiang,Yuxin Chen,Yongsen Pan,Zheng Hu,Wenqi Fan,Qing Li,Hongyang Wang,Jun Wang,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 A/B Agent，一个闭环 A/B 智能体，用于工业推荐策略优化，通过层次化经验树和多路径 Tree-RAG 组织历史策略，自主生成策略并利用在线 A/B 反馈自我进化，在短视频电商推荐系统中实现 GMV 提升 4.829% 且所有护栏指标均正向增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐策略迭代严重依赖大规模 A/B 实验，专家手动设计、配置、分析、调参，过程繁琐耗时；历史实验知识碎片化，难以系统复用；现有 RAG 智能体虽能检索历史策略，但扁平化组织经验，忽视业务场景、推荐阶段、优化目标、实验上下文的层级关系，导致检索失配、跨场景迁移受限，且无法通过连续 A/B 反馈持续优化策略参数。

Method: 提出 A/B Agent 框架，包含三大核心组件：1) 历史策略知识组织：将历史策略构建为层次化经验树，捕捉业务场景、推荐阶段、优化目标、实验上下文的层级关系；2) 自主目标感知策略生成：采用多路径 Tree-RAG 检索可迁移证据，生成可执行策略；3) 实验引导策略自我进化：持续分析在线 A/B 反馈，指导自主调参并更新经验树，实现闭环自我进化。

Result: 离线和在线大量评估证明有效性，在真实短视频电商推荐系统中 GMV 提升 4.829%，同时所有护栏指标均保持正向增长。

Conclusion: A/B Agent 通过层次化经验树、多路径 Tree-RAG 和闭环自我进化机制，解决了工业推荐策略优化中历史知识碎片化、检索失配、跨场景迁移受限及缺乏持续优化的问题，显著提升了业务指标并保障了护栏指标，为工业推荐系统的自动化策略迭代提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Industrial recommendation strategy iteration heavily relies on large-scale A/B experimentation. Traditional tuning requires experts to repeatedly design strategies, configure experiments, analyze results, and adjust parameters, making the process labor-intensive and time-consuming. Meanwhile, valuable knowledge from historical experiments is often fragmented, making systematic reuse difficult through manual expert effort alone. Existing RAG agents partially alleviate this burden by retrieving prior strategies, but typically organize experience in a flat manner, overlooking the hierarchical relationships among business scenarios, recommendation stages, optimization objectives, and experimental contexts. This often results in mismatched retrieval and limited cross-scenario transfer, while preventing agents from continuously refining strategies and parameters through sequential A/B feedback. %
To address these limitations, we propose A/B Agent, a closed-loop A/B agent for industrial recommendation strategy optimization. The framework comprises three tightly coupled core components: Historical Strategy Knowledge Organization, Autonomous Target-Aware Strategy Generation, and Experiment-Guided Strategy Self-Evolution. It organizes historical strategies into a hierarchical experience tree, retrieves transferable evidence through multi-path Tree-RAG to generate executable strategies, and continuously analyzes online A/B feedback to guide autonomous tuning and update the experience tree for self-evolution. Extensive offline and online evaluations demonstrate its effectiveness, including a 4.829% improvement in GMV in a real-world short-video e-commerce recommendation system while maintaining positive gains across all guardrail metrics.

</details>


### [177] [AI Literacy for Legal Translation: Developing Digital Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04641)
*Łucja Biel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨生成式AI对法律翻译的影响，提出面向法律翻译专业的AI素养框架，包含基础、程序、批判、战略四个相互强化的维度，并讨论教学启示与课堂活动设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI正在改变法律翻译领域，带来机遇的同时也引入语言、技术、法律、伦理和认知等多重风险，因此需要针对法律翻译专业构建AI素养框架，以延伸专业胜任力并培养数字韧性。

Method: 采用理论分析与框架构建相结合的方法：首先考察AI对专业法律翻译的影响，进而提出四维AI素养框架，并将AI素养概念化为法律翻译胜任力的横向组成部分；进一步探讨教学启示，设计课堂活动以培养法律翻译教育中的AI素养。

Result: 构建了包含基础、程序、批判、战略四个维度的AI素养框架，将其定位为法律翻译胜任力的横向要素，能够促进数字韧性；同时提出具体课堂活动，旨在使未来翻译人员能够批判性、负责任地、并符合专业标准地整合AI技术。

Conclusion: AI并未改变法律翻译的根本目标，但要求通过AI素养来延伸专业胜任力。所提出的框架与教学活动有助于法律翻译人员在专业实践中批判性、负责任地应用生成式AI，确保符合专业标准。

Abstract: Generative AI is transforming legal translation by introducing opportunities alongside linguistic, technical, legal, ethical and cognitive risks. This chapter examines the implications of AI for professional legal translation and proposes an AI literacy framework tailored to the profession. It argues that AI does not change the fundamental objectives of legal translation but requires an extension of professional competence through AI literacy. The proposed framework comprises four mutually reinforcing dimensions, foundational, procedural, critical and strategic, and conceptualises AI literacy as a transversal component of legal translation competence that fosters digital resilience. It further discusses the pedagogical implications of this framework by proposing classroom activities designed to develop AI literacy in legal translator education, enabling future translators to integrate AI critically, responsibly and in accordance with professional standards.

</details>


### [178] [Calibrating Artificial Guilt: Neurally Grounded Reward Shaping for Prosocial Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04663)
*Aaditya Mehta,Arya Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 利用人类神经与行为数据校准“内疚”信号，将其作为亲社会奖励塑形的权重，在双智能体社会彩票环境中训练PPO智能体，发现神经校准的智能体最接近人类的社会安全选择率，表明人类神经行为先验可作为亲社会奖励塑形的定量约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合作多智能体强化学习常向个体奖励添加社会项，但其尺度通常手工选择。研究探讨能否从人类神经和行为数据中校准内疚信号并迁移至人工智能体。

Method: 使用公开SoDec责任fMRI数据集（40名参与者），拟合主体固定效应回归，以瞬时幸福感变化为因变量，结果类型计数为自变量，恢复内疚权重为“伙伴负面减去社会负面”对比（ŵ=1.118，Cohen's d=0.214）。将该权重嵌入双智能体社会彩票环境，在四种塑形机制下训练独立PPO演员-评论家：神经校准、均匀常数、零（自私）、单位系数神谕。

Result: 在每种条件下1000个评估回合中，神经校准智能体最贴近人类社会安全选择率（0.459 对比人类0.484；KL=0.0012），其余三种条件的KL散度偏离达一到三个数量级。

Conclusion: 人类神经行为先验可作为亲社会奖励塑形的定量约束。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning often adds social terms to individual rewards, yet the scale of those terms is usually chosen by hand. We ask whether a guilt signal can instead be calibrated from human neural and behavioural data and transferred to artificial agents. Using the public SoDec responsibility fMRI dataset (40 participants), we fit a subject-fixed-effects regression of momentary-happiness changes on outcome-type counts and recover a guilt weight as the Partner-negative minus Social-negative contrast ($\hat{w}=1.118$, Cohen's $d=0.214$). We embed this weight in a two-agent Social Lottery environment and train independent Proximal Policy Optimization actor-critics under four shaping regimes: neurally calibrated, uniform constant, zero (selfish), and a unit-coefficient oracle. Across 1{,}000 evaluation episodes per condition, the calibrated agents track the human Social safe-choice rate most closely ($0.459$ vs.\ human $0.484$; $\mathrm{KL}=0.0012$), while the other three conditions deviate by one to three orders of magnitude in KL. Human neurobehavioural priors can therefore act as quantitative constraints on prosocial reward shaping.

</details>


### [179] [Traceable LLM-Generated Hazard Scenarios for Operational Safety Analysis of Aviation Systems Using ASRS Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04697)
*Cristian Mascia,Roberto Pietrantuono,Daniel Rodriguez,Stefano Russo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于AI的航空运行危险分析方法，利用NASA ASRS数据生成候选危险场景，结合结构化假设与叙事描述，并引入进化溯因推理的混合变体提升正确性和稳定性，评估多种大语言模型及提示策略对生成质量的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空系统运行危险分析需综合考虑天气、管制动作、空域限制、飞机运行和人为因素等交互，区别于飞机系统层面的功能危险评估，现有方法缺乏从历史报告自动生成结构化危险场景的能力。

Method: 基于NASA ASRS报告，给定目标不良后果，生成包含分类因素的结构化假设和一致的操作事件叙事序列，每个场景附带基于历史共现证据的合理性评分及与最相似留出报告的可追溯性。进一步提出混合变体：以进化溯因推理产出的结构化假设为条件生成叙事，提升正确性并降低变异性。实验对比多种大语言模型、零样本与少样本提示、以及可选微调，度量提示与模型选择对生成结构和叙事的效度与真实感的影响。

Result: 混合变体显著提高生成场景的正确性并减少变异性；不同模型、提示策略及微调对结构化假设和叙事的效度、真实感有显著影响，少样本提示和微调普遍优于零样本。

Conclusion: AI辅助方法能从ASRS数据高效生成具可追溯性和合理性评分的运行危险场景，进化溯因推理与叙事生成的结合增强了输出质量，为航空安全风险分析提供可扩展的自动化工具。

Abstract: Operational hazard analysis of aviation system operations must consider interactions among weather, ATC actions, airspace constraints, aircraft operations, and human factors - distinct from the functional hazard assessment applied at the aircraft-system level. We present an AI-assisted approach that generates candidate hazard scenarios from NASA's Aviation Safety Reporting System (ASRS). Given a target adverse outcome, it produces a structured hypothesis as categorical factors and a narrative scenario describing an operational event sequence consistent with the structure. Each scenario includes by a plausibility score from historical co-occurrence evidence and traceability to the most similar held-out ASRS reports. We then propose a hybrid variant, conditioning narrative generation on a structured hypothesis produced via evolutionary abduction, improving correctness and reducing variability. We evaluate multiple large language models, zero-shot versus few-shot prompting, and optional fine-tuning, measuring how prompting and model choice affect the validity and realism of the generated structures and narratives.

</details>


### [180] [Diagnosing Tool-Selection Reasoning in LLM Agents with Canary Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04719)
*Atul Anand,Sourav Chattaraj*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出canary tools（金丝雀工具）作为诊断探针，植入智能体的MCP工具集中，通过六类分类法（语义诱饵、参数陷阱、能力幻影、前提盲区、时间诱饵、粒度陷阱）将单一的“选错工具”结果转化为模型工具推理的多维画像。在8个模型、120个任务、多种密度条件下的8,640次实验中发现：1) 模型能力越强，易感性显著下降（CSR跨度约36倍）；2) 能力层级不能单独预测安全性，中端托管模型最易感，同一供应商下廉价模型反而可能更安全；3) 分类法按能力分层：能力幻影最能捕捉前沿模型，其他类型主要捕捉小型开源模型。软化诱导短语后前沿模型CSR基本不变，证明探针测量的是推理而非短语识别。易感性与任务失败负相关（Spearman ρ=-0.34），最稳健模型不受canary压力显著影响。框架、模式、任务和日志已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估只能判断模型选错了工具，无法解释为什么选错。需要一种诊断性方法，深入剖析模型在工具选择推理中的具体弱点，而非仅给出二元的对错结果。

Method: 设计六类canary tools（语义诱饵、参数陷阱、能力幻影、前提盲区、时间诱饵、粒度陷阱），植入MCP工具集作为诊断探针。选取8个模型（6个托管、2个8B开源），跨越三个能力层级，在120个任务上，三种canary密度条件、三个随机种子共8,640次运行，外加2,880次细微消融实验。任务成功由供应商无关的裁判评分，经第二独立裁判验证（Cohen's kappa=0.75）。

Result: 三大发现：1) 易感性随模型能力急剧下降：人均任务CSR在模型间跨度约36倍，Claude Opus 4.8最低，Llama 3.1 8B最高。2) 能力层级不等于安全性：最易感的是中端托管模型，同一供应商下更便宜的模型有时更安全。3) 分类法按能力分层：能力幻影可靠捕捉前沿模型，其余类型对强模型基本无效但对小开源模型触发，实现按能力而非弱度的区分。软化诱导短语后前沿模型CSR不变，证实测量的是推理而非短语匹配。CSR与任务失败负相关（ρ=-0.34），最稳健模型不受canary压力显著降级。

Conclusion: Canary tools框架将工具选择错误转化为可解释的多维推理画像，揭示了模型能力、安全性与工具推理弱点的复杂关系，证明了诊断性探针优于简单准确率评估，为构建更可靠的工具使用智能体提供了量化基准和开源工具链。

Abstract: Agent evaluations tell us that a model picked the wrong tool, but rarely why. We introduce canary tools: diagnostic probe tools planted in an agent's Model Context Protocol (MCP) tool set, each engineered to probe one specific tool-selection weakness. A six-type taxonomy (semantic decoys, parameter traps, capability mirages, prerequisite blindness, temporal decoys, and granularity traps) turns a single "wrong tool" outcome into a multi-dimensional profile of how a model reasons about tools. We evaluate eight models -- six hosted and two 8B open-weight -- spanning three capability tiers, on 120 tasks across three canary-density conditions and three seeds (8,640 runs), plus a 2,880-run subtlety ablation. Task success is graded by a provider-independent judge, corroborated by a second independent judge (Cohen's kappa = 0.75). We report three findings. First, susceptibility drops sharply as models get more capable: the per-task canary susceptibility rate (CSR) ranges about 36x across models, lowest for Claude Opus 4.8 and highest for Llama 3.1 8B. Second, capability tier alone does not predict safety: the most susceptible hosted model is mid-tier, and within a provider the cheaper model can be the safer one. Third, the taxonomy is capability-stratified: capability mirages most reliably trap frontier models, while the other types are largely inert on strong models but fire on small open models, so they discriminate by capability rather than being weak. Softening each canary's give-away phrase leaves frontier CSR essentially unchanged, evidence that the probes measure reasoning, not phrase-spotting. Susceptibility also predicts task failure (Spearman rho = -0.34), while the most robust models are not significantly degraded by canary pressure. We release the framework, canary schemas, tasks, and logs.

</details>


### [181] [When Prompts Become Pixels: Prompt-Region Grounding for Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04726)
*Yongxin Wang,Ruizhe Zhou,Yueling Tang,Yingying Zhu,Xuemin Zhao,Xiaojun Chang,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出可视化任务语义(VTS)基准，将问题嵌入图像中评估多模态大语言模型处理像素级指令的能力，发现所有模型性能显著下降（平均17.8分），揭示了超越OCR的语义通道鸿沟；提出提示区域定地方法，通过对齐问题区域与文本语义并从掩码视图恢复干净表示，在相同训练成本下将四基准VTS准确率从58.0提升至66.3，且无需推理时OCR或区域元数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常以文本形式提供问题，无法评估模型在任务指令以像素形式呈现时的推理能力；实验发现模型虽能正确转录视觉问题却无法有效利用，暴露了读取文本与将其作为推理指令定地之间的语义通道鸿沟。

Method: 提出提示区域定地(prompt-region grounding)，核心设计包括：将问题区域与类型化语义对齐，并从掩码视图中恢复其干净表示。该方法在训练阶段引入区域定地监督，推理时无需OCR或区域元数据即可实现视觉指令的语义定地。

Result: 跨六个MLLM和四个基准的24个模型-任务对中，VTS准确率平均下降17.8分；提出方法在相同训练成本下将四基准VTS平均准绳从58.0提升至66.3（+8.3分），同时保持原始文本接口准确率不变。

Conclusion: 读取任务承载文本并将其作为推理指令进行定地是两种不同的能力；提示区域定地有效缩小了视觉指令与文本指令之间的语义通道鸿沟，为多模态模型处理像素级指令提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models increasingly reason over screenshots and documents where the task itself may be written in pixels. Yet benchmarks usually place questions in text, leaving it unclear whether models use the same instruction equally well across channels. We introduce Visualized Task Semantics (VTS), a controlled intervention that moves the question into the image while keeping the source problem and answer fixed. Across six MLLMs and four benchmarks, accuracy drops in all 24 model-task pairs, by 17.8 points on average. Models often transcribe the visual question correctly yet fail to use it, exposing a semantic channel gap beyond OCR. To reduce this gap, we present prompt-region grounding, whose core design aligns the question region with typed semantics and recovers its clean representation from a masked view. At matched training cost, our method raises four-benchmark VTS accuracy from 58.0 to 66.3 while preserving accuracy on the original interface, and requires no OCR or region metadata at inference. Reading task-bearing text and grounding it as an instruction for reasoning are distinct capabilities.

</details>


### [182] [Chain-of-Thought Monitoring Can Be Unreliable in Implicit-Influence Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04735)
*Agatha Duzan,Asa Cooper Stickland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基准，对比显式影响和隐式影响两种设置下 CoT 监控的可监控性，发现显式影响下监控检出率 60-94%，而隐式影响下检出率大幅下降 41-46 个百分点，且现实系统提示词的添加会进一步将隐式检出率压低至 5% 甚至更低，同时保持行为偏移。这表明以显式影响评估得到的可监控性估计可能高估实际部署中的监控效果，且善意的部署选择可能进一步降低可监控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CoT 监控评估多集中于显式影响设置（提示词直接指令模型隐藏行为），但实际部署中模型常受隐式影响（任务或上下文特征在无明确指令情况下塑造行为），如无关细节导致的招聘评分偏见。因此需要直接对比两种制度下的可监控性，以了解真实部署场景中监控效果是否被高估。

Method: 构建基准测试，涵盖四种任务格式（二选一、数值评分、多选问答、开放式编码）和七种前沿扩展思维模型。引入“轻推”机制：以随口一提方式（隐式）或直接指令并要求隐瞒（显式）传递倾向特定选项的提示。对比两种设置下 CoT 监控器对行为偏移的检出率，并测试开发者常用的系统提示词添加对隐式检出率的影响。

Result: 显式影响下，CoT 监控器检出行为偏移 60-94%，即使模型被指令隐瞒仍会泄露指令到 CoT。隐式影响下，相同因素仍导致行为偏移，但在四个设置中有两个的检出率下降 41-46 个百分点。加入现实系统提示词（旨在减少离题偏见）后，隐式检出率进一步降至 5% 甚至更低，而行为影响本身保持不变。

Conclusion: 显式影响设置下的可监控性估计可能高估实际部署中的监控效果；且旨在改善模型行为的善意部署选择（如添加系统提示词）可能进一步降低隐式影响下的可监控性。这提示需在更真实的隐式影响场景中评估和改进 CoT 监控方法。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is increasingly treated as an important safety layer for frontier reasoning models. Most monitorability evaluations study explicit-influence settings: setups where the prompt directly incentivizes the model to hide something, e.g., by instructing it to perform a hidden side-task. A complementary axis for CoT-monitor evaluations is implicit-influence settings, where the prompt contains no instruction to hide, but the model's behavior is still shaped by features of the task or context, e.g. an irrelevant detail about a candidate that biases a hiring rating.
  We introduce the first benchmark that directly compares CoT monitorability under the two regimes. We test how model behavior changes in the presence of a nudge to choose a particular option. The nudge is delivered either as a casual aside (implicit), or as a direct instruction to act on the nudge and to conceal having done so (explicit). The benchmark spans four task formats (binary choice, numeric rating, multiple-choice QA, open-ended coding) and seven frontier extended-thinking models.
  Under explicit influence, a CoT monitor detects 60-94% of behavior shifts: even models instructed to conceal it leak the instruction into their CoT. Under implicit influence, the same factors still shift behavior, but detection falls by 41-46 percentage points in two of our four settings. Realistic system-prompt additions (of the kind a developer might deploy to reduce off-topic bias) lower implicit detection further, to as low as 5%, while preserving the behavioral influence itself.
  These results suggest that monitorability estimates obtained in explicit-influence settings may over-estimate monitorability, and that monitorability can be further decreased by well-intentioned deployment choices. Our benchmark and code are available at https://github.com/agatha-duzan/implicit-vs-explicit-influence

</details>


### [183] [EviGraph: Evidence-Guided Autonomous Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04738)
*Zhenjiang Ren,Ruiji Li,Xujing Zhang,Ziliang Pang,Shuo Ren,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EviGraph通过证据图显式维护声明-证据结构，实现自主研究的可靠性与一致性显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主研究代理将研究组织为顺序管道，缺乏对跨阶段演化的声明-证据结构的显式维护和验证，导致输出常包含无依据声明以及研究问题、实验、结果、结论间的不一致。

Method: 提出EviGraph框架，将研究过程建模为包含Problem、Gap、Hypothesis、Experiment、Finding、Claim节点的类型化证据图，作为代理的操作状态；引入证据链检查机制，检测缺失依赖、语义错位和结果-声明不一致，定位最早弱节点并重新生成受影响的下游子图；采用图检查点防止修复失败污染已验证证据；仅在所有保留声明都有验证证据链支撑时生成手稿。

Result: 在ARC-Bench-ML和NanoResearch-20基准上，EviGraph整体研究性能优于端到端基线，声明支撑率比最强基线提升40.19%，实验数据一致性达到87.73%。

Conclusion: EviGraph通过显式维护和验证证据图中的声明-证据结构，显著提升了自主研究代理的可靠性和一致性。

Abstract: Autonomous research agents can generate hypotheses, execute experiments, and draft manuscripts, yet their outputs often contain unsupported claims and inconsistencies between research questions, experiments, results, and conclusions. We argue that this problem is partly architectural: existing systems organize research as sequential pipelines but do not explicitly maintain or validate the evolving claim-evidence structure across stages.In this paper, we introduce EviGraph, an autonomous research framework that represents the research process as a typed evidence graph containing Problem, Gap, Hypothesis, Experiment, Finding, and Claim nodes. The graph serves as the operational state of the agent rather than a post-hoc record. EviGraph inspects evidence chains for missing dependencies, semantic misalignment, and result-claim inconsistencies, localizes the earliest weak node, and regenerates its affected downstream subgraph. Graph checkpointing prevents unsuccessful repairs from corrupting previously validated evidence. Manuscripts are generated only after every retained claim is grounded in a validated evidence chain.Experiments on ARC-Bench-ML and NanoResearch-20 show that EviGraph outperforms the compared end-to-end research-agent baselines in overall research performance, improves Claim Support Rate by 40.19% over the strongest baseline, and achieves 87.73% Experimental Data Consistency. These results demonstrate the value of explicit evidence-state maintenance for reliable autonomous research.

</details>


### [184] [Fewer Tokens, Smaller Cache: Reward-Coordinated Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04771)
*Qiyuan Zhu,Dezhi Li,Pengyu Cheng,Tianle Chen,Jiacheng Wang,Ruijie Shen,Hao Gu,Sida Lin,Zirui Liu,Jiacheng Liu,Sirui Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReCo通过过程奖励协调KV缓存压缩与生成控制，在大幅降低推理开销（生成token减少37%-65%，延迟降低2.08-2.35倍）的同时基本保持推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（LRMs）依赖长链式思维（CoT）推理，但冗长的中间步骤导致严重过度思考，推高推理成本。现有KV缓存压缩方法采用统一策略，且仅关注缓存移除端。两大观察驱动新思路：1）推理状态对上下文丢失的容忍度随轨迹变化，过程奖励可追踪该变化——在高奖励步骤删除token比随机删除更能保持准确率；2）压缩非生成端免费，更小缓存会诱导模型生成更多token，部分抵消节省。

Method: 提出ReCo（奖励协调压缩），一种逐步框架：轻量级过程奖励估计器为每个完成步骤打分，驱动三组件：① 奖励自适应KV缓存压缩——高奖励步骤更激进压缩，低奖励步骤保留更多；② 奖励分带反思token惩罚——抑制冗余生成；③ 基于置信度的早停——推理可靠时提前终止。

Result: 在三种推理模型与六个基准上，ReCo相对Full CoT使生成token减少37%-65%，端到端延迟降低2.08-2.35倍，准确率基本无损。

Conclusion: 通过过程奖励统一协调缓存压缩与生成控制，ReCo有效缓解LRM的过度思考问题，实现推理效率与准确率的双赢。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) excel on complex tasks through long chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, but their lengthy intermediate steps cause severe overthinking that inflates inference cost. KV-cache compression is a common solution, yet existing reasoning-oriented methods apply a uniform policy across the trajectory and judge compression only by what it removes from the cache. Two observations point the other way. First, a reasoning state's tolerance to context loss varies along the trajectory, and process reward tracks it: deleting tokens at high-reward steps preserves accuracy far better than deleting the same budget at random. Second, compression is not free on the generation side, since a smaller cache leads the model to generate more tokens, partly canceling the saving. Together these motivate coordinating both sides under a single process reward. We propose ReCo (Reward-Coordinated Compression), a step-wise framework in which a lightweight process-reward estimator scores each completed step and drives three components: (1) reward-adaptive KV-cache compression that shrinks the retained cache harder at high-reward steps and less at low-reward ones, (2) a reward-banded penalty on reflection tokens that curbs redundant generation, and (3) confidence-based early stopping that triggers when the reasoning is reliable. Across three reasoning models and six benchmarks, ReCo reduces generated tokens by 37%-65% and end-to-end latency by 2.08x-2.35x over Full CoT, all while largely preserving accuracy.

</details>


### [185] [NSF-HRPT: Neural Semantic Field meets Hierarchical Risk Perception Tree for Safety-Critical Scenario Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04776)
*Yu Zhao,Jiangyu Pan,Tao Hu,Ming Yin,Fan Yang,Jiangfan Liu,Xiubo Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出NSF-HRPT框架，结合神经语义场(NSF)与分层风险感知树(HRPT)，实现从单目视觉输入的定量风险评估，并通过Sim2Real增强提升真实场景适用性，在合成基准上达到SOTA，在真实数据集上接近SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶碰撞预测研究虽有进展，但难以从单目视觉准确量化风险等级，主要挑战在于多智能体交互的复杂动态和真实环境的固有不确定性。

Method: 提出NSF-HRPT框架：1) 神经语义场(NSF)从仿真数据学习场景语义、轨迹预测和概率TTC分布；2) 分层风险感知树(HRPT)利用预训练NSF作为先验，实现高效并行计算和多智能体风险空间推理；3) Sim2Real增强策略引入基础模型先验，无需重新训练即可提升真实世界适用性。

Result: 在合成基准上达到最先进性能；在真实世界数据集上，TTC估计精度和风险定位精度均达到接近最先进水平。

Conclusion: 该方法为单目相机输入的实时风险感知提供了有效解决方案，结合学习感知与结构化推理，兼顾准确性和计算效率。

Abstract: The ability to accurately assess and anticipate risks in safety-critical scenarios is crucial for autonomous driving systems. While existing research has made progress in collision prediction, accurately quantifying risk levels from monocular vision inputs remains challenging due to the complex dynamics of multi-agent interactions and the inherent uncertainty in real-world environments. To address these challenges, we present NSF-HRPT, a novel framework that combines learning-based perception with structured reasoning for quantitative risk assessment. Our approach features a Neural Semantic Field (NSF) that learns to model scene semantics, trajectory predictions, and probabilistic Time-to-Collision (TTC) distributions from simulation data. During inference, the pre-trained NSF serves as a prior for our Hierarchical Risk Perception Tree (HRPT), which enables efficient parallel computation and spatial reasoning about multi-agent risks. Additionally, we introduce a Sim2Real enhancement strategy that improves real-world applicability without retraining by incorporating priors from foundation models. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our framework achieves state-of-the-art performance on synthetic benchmarks and delivers competitive, near-state-of-the-art results on real-world datasets for both TTC estimation accuracy and risk localization precision. The proposed method provides an effective solution for real-time risk awareness from monocular camera inputs.

</details>
